\n    Search ChromaDB for Census tables matching a concept.\n    Returns table metadata including supported geographies.\n    \n    Examples:\n    - "Find tables about median income"\n    - "What tables have population data?"\n    \ncensus_api_call - \n    Execute a Census API query and fetch actual data.\n    \n    Supports complex Census API patterns including:\n    - Multiple dataset categories (acs/acs5, acs/acs5/subject, acs/acs1/profile, etc.)\n    - year: Census year (required)\n    - dataset: Dataset path like "acs/acs5", "acs/acs5/subject", "acs/acs1/profile" (required)\n    - variables: List of variable codes OR "group(TABLE_CODE)" for subject tables (required)\n    - geo_for: Geography for clause dict like {"county": "*", "state (or part)": "*"} (required)\n    - geo_in: Geography in clause dict(s) - can be complex like {"state": "06", "metropolitan statistical area/micropolitan statistical area": "35620"} (optional)\n    - geo_for: Geography for clause like "county:*" or complex {"county": "*", "state (or part)": "*"} (required)\n    - geo_in: Geography in clause like "state:06" or complex multi-level dict (optional)\n    - use_groups: Boolean - use group(table_code) syntax for subject/profile tables (optional, auto-detected)\n    - variables: Override default variables with custom list (optional)\n\n    Examples:\n    - {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5", "table_code": "B01003", "geo_for": "county:*", "geo_in": "state:06"}\n    - {"year": 2023, "dataset": "acs/acs5/subject", "table_code": "S0101", "geo_for": "state:*", "use_groups": true}\n    -
